    27	/** @param {string} iso */
    28	const tsForFile = (iso) => iso.replace(/[:.]/g, '-');
    29	
    30	/** @param {ReturnType<typeof bundlePaths>} p */
    31	const markerPath = (p) => `${p.dir}/rotation.marker.json`;
    32	
    33	/**
    34	 * Reconcile a leftover rotation marker (crash recovery, part of load). The
    35	 * mutations run under the repo lock, acquired WITHOUT waiting (gate-2 minor
    36	 * 16): a held lock means someone is mid-operation — reconciliation defers to
    37	 * the next unlocked load instead of writing behind their back.
    38	 * @param {any} io @param {ReturnType<typeof bundlePaths>} p @param {string[]} warnings @param {string} root
    39	 */
    40	function reconcileMarker(io, p, warnings, root) {
    41	  const mp = markerPath(p);
    42	  if (!io.fs.existsSync(mp)) return;
    43	  try {
    44	    withLock(root, { ...io, lockRetry: { attempts: 0, delayMs: 0 } }, () => {
    45	      if (!io.fs.existsSync(mp)) return; // a competitor reconciled while we acquired
    46	      const marker = safeReadJson(io.fs, mp);
    47	      if (!marker.ok) {
    48	        io.fs.unlinkSync(mp);
    49	        warnings.push('rotation marker was unparseable — rolled the rotation back (marker cleared, journal preserved)');
    50	        return;
    51	      }
    52	      const stem = `${tsForFile(marker.value.startedAt)}.${marker.value.kind}`;
    53	      const freeze = `${p.historyDir}/${stem}.json`;
    54	      const rotated = `${p.historyDir}/${stem}.ndjson`;
    55	
    56	      if (io.fs.existsSync(freeze)) {
    57	        // Roll forward: finish whichever step the crash interrupted.
    58	        if (io.fs.existsSync(p.journal) && !io.fs.existsSync(rotated)) {
    59	          io.fs.renameSync(p.journal, rotated);
    60	        }
    61	        if (!io.fs.existsSync(p.journal)) {
    62	          atomicWriteText(io.fs, p.journal, '');
    63	        }
    64	        io.fs.unlinkSync(mp);
    65	        warnings.push(`completed an interrupted rotation (roll-forward of ${stem}); marker cleared`);
    66	      } else {
    67	        io.fs.unlinkSync(mp);
    68	        warnings.push(`aborted an incomplete rotation (roll-back; ${stem} freeze never landed); marker cleared, journal preserved`);
    69	      }
    70	    });
    71	  } catch (err) {
    72	    if (err instanceof LockHeldError) {
    73	      warnings.push('a rotation marker is present but the repo lock is held — reconciliation deferred to the next unlocked load');
    74	      return;
    75	    }
    76	    throw err;
    77	  }
    78	}
    79	
    80	/**
    81	 * @param {any} io @param {ReturnType<typeof bundlePaths>} p @param {string[]} warnings
    82	 * @returns {any | null} a usable base bundle, or null when nothing exists
    83	 */
    84	function resolveBase(io, p, warnings) {
    85	  const snapshotExists = io.fs.existsSync(p.snapshot);
    86	  if (snapshotExists) {
    87	    const snap = safeReadJson(io.fs, p.snapshot);
    88	    if (snap.ok && validateBundle(snap.value).ok) return snap.value;
    89	    warnings.push(
    90	      snap.ok
    91	        ? 'snapshot bundle.json failed schema validation — attempting recovery'
    92	        : `snapshot bundle.json is unreadable (${snap.error}) — attempting recovery`,
    93	    );
    94	    const bak = safeReadJson(io.fs, p.bak);
    95	    if (bak.ok && validateBundle(bak.value).ok) {
    96	      warnings.push('recovered from backup bundle.json.bak; replaying journal past its journalSeq');
    97	      return bak.value;
    98	    }
    99	    if (io.fs.existsSync(p.bak)) warnings.push('backup bundle.json.bak is also unusable');
   100	    if (io.fs.existsSync(p.journal)) {
   101	      warnings.push('rebuilding the bundle by replaying the whole journal over a fresh seed (recovery of last resort)');
   102	      return emptyBundle({ platform: 'unknown', model: 'unknown', goal: 'unknown (rebuilt from journal)' }, io.now());
   103	    }
   104	    return null;
   105	  }
   106	  if (io.fs.existsSync(p.journal)) {
   107	    warnings.push('no snapshot present — rebuilding the bundle from the journal');
   108	    return emptyBundle({ platform: 'unknown', model: 'unknown', goal: 'unknown (rebuilt from journal)' }, io.now());
   109	  }
   110	  return null;
   111	}
   112	
   113	/**
   114	 * Load the active bundle with full crash recovery. Read path — not lock-gated;
   115	 * rotation-marker reconciliation is part of load. A managed tree that fails
   116	 * the symlink/realpath jail (gate-2 fix 6) is refused outright: bundle null,
   117	 * `unsafe: true`, and the problem in warnings — callers must not treat this as
   118	 * "no bundle yet" and seed through the link.
   119	 * @param {string} root @param {any} io
   120	 * @returns {{bundle: any | null, warnings: string[], unsafe?: boolean}}
   121	 */
   122	export function loadBundle(root, io) {
   123	  const p = bundlePaths(root);
   124	  /** @type {string[]} */
   125	  const warnings = [];
   126	  if (!io.fs.existsSync(p.dir)) return { bundle: null, warnings };
   127	
   128	  const safe = checkHandoffTree(root, io);
   129	  if (!safe.ok) return { bundle: null, warnings: [safe.problem], unsafe: true };
   130	
   131	  reconcileMarker(io, p, warnings, root);
   132	
   133	  // An interrupted purge is visible on every read path (gate-2 minor 16): the
   134	  // tree may still hold transcript copies until purge-transcript finishes.
   135	  if (io.fs.existsSync(`${p.dir}/purge.marker.json`)) {
   136	    warnings.push('an interrupted purge left its marker — run baton purge-transcript to finish scrubbing every retained copy');
   137	  }
   138	
   139	  let bundle = resolveBase(io, p, warnings);
   140	  if (bundle === null) return { bundle: null, warnings };
   141	
   142	  if (io.fs.existsSync(p.journal)) {
   143	    const { entries, warnings: jw } = readAllTolerant(io.fs, p.journal);
   144	    for (const w of jw) {
   145	      warnings.push(`journal line ${w.line}: ${w.kind}${w.kind === 'seq-order' ? ` (prev ${w.prev}, got ${w.seq})` : ''}`);
   146	    }
   147	    for (const e of entries) {
   148	      // Envelope guard (gate-2 iter-2 B4): a parseable but non-object line
   149	      // (null, number, bare string) is not an event — skip it with a warning
   150	      // rather than crash replay on `e.seq` / `e.dedupeKey`.
   151	      if (e === null || typeof e !== 'object' || Array.isArray(e)) {
   152	        warnings.push('skipped a malformed journal line (not an event envelope)');
   153	        continue;
   154	      }
   155	      if (typeof e.seq === 'number' && e.seq <= bundle.journalSeq) continue;
   156	      bundle = applyEvent(bundle, e);
   157	    }
   158	  }
   159	  return { bundle, warnings };
   160	}
   161	
   162	/**
   163	 * Lock-context snapshot write: caller already holds the repo lock and passes
   164	 * its fencing token; the write is fence-checked immediately before landing
   165	 * (the fast-abort layer — gate-2 fix, reviewer-b finding 12).
   166	 * @param {string} root @param {any} bundle @param {any} io @param {string} token
   167	 */
   168	export function writeSnapshotIn(root, bundle, io, token) {
   169	  const p = bundlePaths(root);
   170	  return guardedWrite(root, io, token, () => {
   171	    assertHandoffTreeSafe(root, io);
   172	    ensureDir(io.fs, p.dir);
   173	    // Size discipline runs in the write path itself (gate-2 fix 10): every
   174	    // persisted snapshot is within budget; identity for in-budget bundles.
   175	    const bounded = compact(bundle);
   176	    // Validate-before-backup (gate-2 major 15): a corrupt current snapshot
   177	    // must never overwrite a good .bak — the backup only rotates when the
   178	    // outgoing snapshot itself would be recoverable.
   179	    const prev = safeReadJson(io.fs, p.snapshot);
   180	    if (prev.ok && validateBundle(prev.value).ok) {
   181	      atomicWriteText(io.fs, p.bak, io.fs.readFileSync(p.snapshot, 'utf8'));
   182	    }
   183	    atomicWriteText(io.fs, p.snapshot, JSON.stringify(bounded, null, 2) + '\n');
   184	    atomicWriteText(io.fs, p.handoffMd, renderHandoffMd(bounded));
   185	  });
   186	}
   187	
   188	/**
   189	 * Persist a snapshot: back up the previous good one, write atomically, render
   190	 * HANDOFF.md alongside. Mutation — runs under the repo lock.
   191	 * @param {string} root @param {any} bundle @param {any} io
   192	 */
   193	export function writeSnapshot(root, bundle, io) {
   194	  return withLock(root, io, (token) => writeSnapshotIn(root, bundle, io, token));
   195	}
   196	
   197	/**
   198	 * Append one journal entry under the repo lock. A pre-numbered entry keeps its
   199	 * seq; a seq-less entry is allocated max(snapshot.journalSeq, journal tail) + 1.
   200	 * @param {string} root @param {any} entry @param {any} io
   201	 * @returns {number} the entry's (possibly allocated) seq
   202	 */
   203	export function appendJournal(root, entry, io) {
   204	  const p = bundlePaths(root);
   205	  return withLock(root, io, (token) =>
   206	    guardedWrite(root, io, token, () => {
   207	      assertHandoffTreeSafe(root, io);
   208	      ensureDir(io.fs, p.dir);
   209	      let seq = entry.seq;
   210	      let toAppend = entry;
   211	      if (typeof seq !== 'number') {
   212	        const snap = safeReadJson(io.fs, p.snapshot);
   213	        const snapSeq = snap.ok && typeof snap.value?.journalSeq === 'number' ? snap.value.journalSeq : 0;
   214	        let tailSeq = 0;
   215	        if (io.fs.existsSync(p.journal)) {
   216	          for (const e of readAllTolerant(io.fs, p.journal).entries) {
   217	            if (typeof e.seq === 'number' && e.seq > tailSeq) tailSeq = e.seq;
   218	          }
   219	        }
   220	        seq = Math.max(snapSeq, tailSeq) + 1;

     1	import { atomicWriteJson } from '../util/fsx.mjs';
     2	import { appendEntry, readAllTolerant } from '../util/jsonl.mjs';
     3	import { dedupeKey } from '../util/ids.mjs';
     4	import { checkHandoffTree } from '../util/jail.mjs';
     5	
     6	/**
     7	 * Operation-scoped mutation lock (plan §Concurrency, Lock model).
     8	 * Acquire = atomic non-recursive mkdir of .handoff/lock/; exclusivity comes from
     9	 * EEXIST. Ownership facts live in owner.json; logical session ownership lives in
    10	 * bundle.json, never here. Takeover only when the owner is provably dead on this
    11	 * host — every other contended state refuses, and `force` never changes that.
    12	 */
    13	
    14	export class LockHeldError extends Error {
    15	  constructor(/** @type {string} */ message) {
    16	    super(message);
    17	    this.name = 'LockHeldError';
    18	  }
    19	}
    20	
    21	export class FencingError extends Error {
    22	  constructor(/** @type {string} */ message) {
    23	    super(message);
    24	    this.name = 'FencingError';
    25	  }
    26	}
    27	
    28	/** @param {string} root */
    29	function lockPaths(root) {
    30	  const dir = `${root}/.handoff`;
    31	  return {
    32	    dir,
    33	    lockDir: `${dir}/lock`,
    34	    owner: `${dir}/lock/owner.json`,
    35	    journal: `${dir}/journal.ndjson`,
    36	  };
    37	}
    38	
    39	const ESCAPE_HATCH =
    40	  'Verify manually that no baton process still holds it, then delete .handoff/lock to clear.';
    41	
    42	/**
    43	 * Inspect the current lock state.
    44	 * @param {any} io @param {ReturnType<typeof lockPaths>} p
    45	 * @returns {{state: 'free'} | {state: 'torn', cause: string} | {state: 'live'|'dead'|'cross-host', owner: any}}
    46	 */
    47	function inspect(io, p) {
    48	  if (!io.fs.existsSync(p.lockDir)) return { state: 'free' };
    49	  if (!io.fs.existsSync(p.owner)) {
    50	    return { state: 'torn', cause: 'owner.json is missing (torn lock metadata)' };
    51	  }
    52	  let owner;
    53	  try {
    54	    owner = JSON.parse(io.fs.readFileSync(p.owner, 'utf8'));
    55	  } catch {
    56	    return { state: 'torn', cause: 'owner.json is unparseable (torn lock metadata)' };
    57	  }
    58	  if (owner.host !== io.host) return { state: 'cross-host', owner };
    59	  if (io.processAlive(owner.pid, owner.startTime)) return { state: 'live', owner };
    60	  return { state: 'dead', owner };
    61	}
    62	
    63	/** @param {any} io @param {ReturnType<typeof lockPaths>} p @param {string} text */
    64	function journalNote(io, p, text) {
    65	  io.fs.mkdirSync(p.dir, { recursive: true });
    66	  // Allocate seq + dedupeKey like every other journal entry (gate-2 fix,
    67	  // reviewer-b finding 2): a keyless, seq-less note poisons journalSeq to NaN
    68	  // on replay and dedupes every later keyless event. We are already inside the
    69	  // lock here, so reading the tail for allocation is race-free.
    70	  let tailSeq = 0;
    71	  try {
    72	    const snap = JSON.parse(io.fs.readFileSync(`${p.dir}/bundle.json`, 'utf8'));
    73	    if (typeof snap?.journalSeq === 'number') tailSeq = snap.journalSeq;
    74	  } catch {
    75	    // no snapshot yet — journal tail below still counts
    76	  }
    77	  try {
    78	    for (const e of readAllTolerant(io.fs, p.journal).entries) {
    79	      if (typeof e.seq === 'number' && e.seq > tailSeq) tailSeq = e.seq;
    80	    }
    81	  } catch {
    82	    // no journal yet
    83	  }
    84	  const ts = io.now();
    85	  appendEntry(io.fs, p.journal, {
    86	    seq: tailSeq + 1,
    87	    ts,
    88	    type: 'note',
    89	    dedupeKey: dedupeKey({ ts, type: 'note', source: 'lock', text }),
    90	    writerId: ['lock', io.host, io.pid].join('-'),
    91	    source: 'lock',
    92	    payload: { text },
    93	  });
    94	}
    95	
    96	/** @param {any} io @param {ReturnType<typeof lockPaths>} p */
    97	function publishOwner(io, p) {
    98	  const token = io.newFencingToken();
    99	  atomicWriteJson(io.fs, p.owner, {
   100	    host: io.host,
   101	    pid: io.pid,
   102	    startTime: io.startTime,
   103	    fencingToken: token,
   104	    acquiredAt: io.now(),
   105	    heartbeatAt: io.now(),
   106	  });
   107	  return token;
   108	}
   109	
   110	/** @param {any} io @param {ReturnType<typeof lockPaths>} p */
   111	function acquire(io, p) {
   112	  io.fs.mkdirSync(p.dir, { recursive: true });
   113	  try {
   114	    io.fs.mkdirSync(p.lockDir);
   115	    return publishOwner(io, p);
   116	  } catch (err) {
   117	    if (/** @type {any} */ (err)?.code !== 'EEXIST') throw err;
   118	  }
   119	
   120	  const s = inspect(io, p);
   121	  switch (s.state) {
   122	    case 'free':
   123	      // Raced a release between mkdir failure and inspection; retry once. A
   124	      // competitor can win THIS mkdir too (gate-2 fix 12: observed under real
   125	      // barrier-released contention) — that is contention, not corruption.
   126	      try {
   127	        io.fs.mkdirSync(p.lockDir);
   128	      } catch (err) {
   129	        if (/** @type {any} */ (err)?.code === 'EEXIST') {
   130	          throw new LockHeldError('lock was re-acquired by a competitor mid-inspection — transient contention');
   131	        }
   132	        throw err;
   133	      }
   134	      return publishOwner(io, p);
   135	    case 'live':
   136	      throw new LockHeldError(
   137	        `lock is held by a live process (pid ${s.owner.pid} on ${s.owner.host}) — terminate that process first.`,
   138	      );
   139	    case 'cross-host':
   140	      throw new Error(
   141	        `lock is held from another host ('${s.owner.host}', this is '${io.host}') — cross-host locks are never provably safe to steal. ${ESCAPE_HATCH}`,
   142	      );
   143	    case 'torn':
   144	      // During ACQUIRE, torn metadata is usually a competitor caught between
   145	      // its mkdir and owner.json publication — transient by construction, so
   146	      // LockHeldError lets withLock wait it out (gate-2 fix 12). A genuinely
   147	      // crashed publisher exhausts the retry budget and surfaces this same
   148	      // message; recovery semantics (recoverLock) are unchanged: never steal.
   149	      throw new LockHeldError(`${s.cause} — unsupported for automatic recovery. ${ESCAPE_HATCH}`);
   150	    case 'dead': {
   151	      // Atomic dead-lock reclaim (gate-2 iter-2 B1, hardened iter-3 finding-1).
   152	      // rm-then-mkdir is NOT mutually exclusive: two contenders can both observe
   153	      // 'dead' before either removes the stale dir, then both rm+mkdir — the
   154	      // second silently clobbers the first, yielding two simultaneous holders and
   155	      // two takeover notes. rename() is the atomic arbiter instead: the stale
   156	      // lock dir has exactly one name, so only one process can move it aside (the
   157	      // rest get ENOENT). Winning the rename IS winning the reclaim; a contender
   158	      // that recreated the dir via the 'free' path first makes our mkdir EEXIST →
   159	      // transient contention. The fencing token stays the write-time backstop.
   160	      const aside = `${p.lockDir}.reclaim-${io.pid}-${io.newFencingToken()}`;
   161	      try {
   162	        io.fs.renameSync(p.lockDir, aside);
   163	      } catch (err) {
   164	        // Source already gone → another contender is reclaiming it; wait+retry.
   165	        if (/** @type {any} */ (err)?.code === 'ENOENT') {
   166	          throw new LockHeldError('a competitor is reclaiming the dead lock — transient contention');
   167	        }
   168	        throw err;
   169	      }
   170	      io.fs.rmSync(aside, { recursive: true, force: true });
   171	      try {
   172	        io.fs.mkdirSync(p.lockDir);
   173	      } catch (err) {
   174	        if (/** @type {any} */ (err)?.code === 'EEXIST') {
   175	          throw new LockHeldError('a competitor re-claimed the dead lock first — transient contention');
   176	        }
   177	        throw err;
   178	      }
   179	      const token = publishOwner(io, p);
   180	      // Journal the takeover only now — we HOLD the lock (fresh dir + published
   181	      // owner), so the note's seq is allocated while holding it (plan
   182	      // §Concurrency "sequence numbers are allocated under the lock"), never
   183	      // racing a free-path competitor's concurrent append (iter-3 F2).
   184	      // Journaling at the earlier rename win ran WITHOUT the lock and could
   185	      // collide seq with a real event → replay drops one. The note is therefore
   186	      // best-effort (0-or-1: lost only when we lose the fresh-mkdir race and
   187	      // throw above); the reclaim itself stays exactly-once via the atomic rename.
   188	      journalNote(io, p, `lock takeover: took over stale lock from dead pid ${s.owner.pid} (start time ${s.owner.startTime})`);
   189	      return token;
   190	    }
   191	    /* c8 ignore next 2 */
   192	    default:
   193	      throw new Error('unreachable lock state');
   194	  }
   195	}
   196	
   197	/**
   198	 * Fence-guarded release: the lock dir is removed only while owner.json still
   199	 * carries OUR token. A holder that paused past a takeover must not delete the
   200	 * competitor's lock on its way out — release is a write like any other.
   201	 * @param {any} io @param {ReturnType<typeof lockPaths>} p @param {string} token
   202	 */
   203	function release(io, p, token) {
   204	  try {
   205	    const current = JSON.parse(io.fs.readFileSync(p.owner, 'utf8'));
   206	    if (current.fencingToken !== token) return; // a competitor took over — their lock, not ours
   207	  } catch {
   208	    return; // owner metadata gone or torn mid-hold — never guess; leave it for recovery
   209	  }
   210	  io.fs.rmSync(p.lockDir, { recursive: true, force: true });
   211	}
   212	
   213	// A live same-host holder is transient by design — operation-scoped locks are
   214	// held for milliseconds — so contention waits instead of dropping the
   215	// operation (gate-2 fix 12: simultaneous checkpoints must all land). The budget
   216	// is generous (≈15 s): under heavy load a writer may be starved of scheduling
   217	// for seconds, and waiting is strictly safer than silently dropping its work.
   218	// Every other contended state (torn, cross-host) stays fail-fast: waiting
   219	// cannot make those provably safe.
   220	const DEFAULT_RETRY = { attempts: 600, delayMs: 25 };
   221	
   222	/** Synchronous sleep without burning a core: Atomics.wait on a throwaway buffer. */
   223	const sleepMs = (/** @type {number} */ ms) => {
   224	  Atomics.wait(new Int32Array(new SharedArrayBuffer(4)), 0, 0, ms);
   225	};
   226	
   227	/**
   228	 * Acquire → run fn(token) → release (also on throw). Synchronous by design:
   229	 * everything the lock guards is sync fs work. Live-held locks are retried on
   230	 * a bounded budget (override via io.lockRetry = {attempts, delayMs}).

   280	  return fn();
   281	}
   282	
   283	/**
   284	 * Explicit recovery. `force` is an intent flag only — it never changes the
   285	 * decision: exactly one state recovers (provably-dead same-host owner).
   286	 * @param {string} root @param {any} io @param {{force?: boolean}} [opts]
   287	 * @returns {{recovered: boolean, refusedReason: string | null}}
   288	 */
   289	export function recoverLock(root, io, opts = {}) {
   290	  void opts;
   291	  const p = lockPaths(root);
   292	  // Jail before recovery (gate-2 iter-2 B2): recoverLock removes the lock dir,
   293	  // so a symlinked managed tree must be refused first — even with force, a
   294	  // symlink is never provably safe to delete through.
   295	  const safe = checkHandoffTree(root, io);
   296	  if (!safe.ok) return { recovered: false, refusedReason: `${safe.problem} — refusing recovery` };
   297	  const s = inspect(io, p);
   298	  switch (s.state) {
   299	    case 'free':
   300	      return { recovered: true, refusedReason: null };
   301	    case 'dead': {
   302	      // Provably-dead owner: removal is justified by the verified state itself,
   303	      // not by holding the fence (there is no live holder to fence against).
   304	      io.fs.rmSync(p.lockDir, { recursive: true, force: true });
   305	      // Journal the recovery under a fresh acquisition of the now-free lock so
   306	      // its seq is allocated while holding the lock (iter-3 F2) — journaling
   307	      // right after the bare rmSync ran without the lock and could collide seq
   308	      // with a writer that acquired in the gap. Best-effort: the recovery has
   309	      // already succeeded, so a note-write failure must not fail it.
   310	      const deadPid = s.owner.pid;
   311	      try {
   312	        withLock(root, io, () => journalNote(io, p, `lock recovery: cleared stale lock of dead pid ${deadPid}`));
   313	      } catch {
   314	        /* audit note is best-effort */
   315	      }
   316	      return { recovered: true, refusedReason: null };
   317	    }
   318	    case 'live':
   319	      return {
   320	        recovered: false,
   321	        refusedReason: `lock is held by a live process (pid ${s.owner.pid} on ${s.owner.host}) — terminate that process first.`,
   322	      };
   323	    case 'cross-host':
   324	      return {
   325	        recovered: false,
   326	        refusedReason: `lock is held from another host ('${s.owner.host}', this is '${io.host}') — cross-host locks are never provably safe to steal, even with force. ${ESCAPE_HATCH}`,
   327	      };
   328	    case 'torn':
   329	      return {
   330	        recovered: false,

   197	/**
   198	 * Append one journal entry under the repo lock. A pre-numbered entry keeps its
   199	 * seq; a seq-less entry is allocated max(snapshot.journalSeq, journal tail) + 1.
   200	 * @param {string} root @param {any} entry @param {any} io
   201	 * @returns {number} the entry's (possibly allocated) seq
   202	 */
   203	export function appendJournal(root, entry, io) {
   204	  const p = bundlePaths(root);
   205	  return withLock(root, io, (token) =>
   206	    guardedWrite(root, io, token, () => {
   207	      assertHandoffTreeSafe(root, io);
   208	      ensureDir(io.fs, p.dir);
   209	      let seq = entry.seq;
   210	      let toAppend = entry;

   213	        const snapSeq = snap.ok && typeof snap.value?.journalSeq === 'number' ? snap.value.journalSeq : 0;

   217	            if (typeof e.seq === 'number' && e.seq > tailSeq) tailSeq = e.seq;
   218	          }
   219	        }
   220	        seq = Math.max(snapSeq, tailSeq) + 1;
   221	        toAppend = { ...entry, seq };
   222	      }
   223	      appendEntry(io.fs, p.journal, toAppend);
   224	      return seq;
   225	    }),
   226	  );
   227	}

    59	  it('a contender that loses the rename sees ENOENT-as-contention, not a second takeover', () => {
    60	    // Simulate the rename losing the race: the stale dir is already gone when we
    61	    // try to move it aside → LockHeldError (retryable), never a duplicate claim.
    62	    const io = makeIo({ files: { [OWNER]: deadOwner }, lockRetry: { attempts: 0, delayMs: 0 } });
    63	    const realRename = io.fs.renameSync.bind(io.fs);
    64	    io.fs.renameSync = (from, to) => {
    65	      if (String(from).endsWith('/lock') && /\/lock\.reclaim-/.test(String(to))) {
    66	        throw Object.assign(new Error('ENOENT'), { code: 'ENOENT' });
    67	      }
    68	      return realRename(from, to);
    69	    };
    70	    assert.throws(() => withLock('/repo', io, (t) => t), /reclaiming the dead lock|transient contention/i);
    71	  });
    72	
    73	  it('after release the lock is free and the next acquisition mints its own token', () => {
    74	    const io = makeIo({ files: { [OWNER]: deadOwner } });
    75	    const first = withLock('/repo', io, (t) => t);
    76	    assert.ok(first, 'first contender takes over the dead lock');
    77	    assert.equal(io.fs.existsSync('/repo/.handoff/lock'), false, 'lock released after the operation');
    78	    const second = withLock('/repo', io, (t) => t);
    79	    assert.ok(second && second !== first, 'the next acquisition gets its own fresh token');
    80	  });
    81	});
    82	
    83	describe('lock/recover refuse a symlinked managed tree before mutating (B2)', () => {
    84	  function symlinkedLockIo() {
    85	    // The lock dir exists inside the managed tree (so the jail walk encounters
    86	    // it) but is a symlink pointing outside the repo: lstat reports a link.
    87	    const io = makeIo({ files: { '/repo/.handoff/bundle.json': '{}', '/repo/.handoff/lock/owner.json': deadOwner } });
    88	    const realLstat = io.fs.lstatSync.bind(io.fs);
    89	    io.fs.lstatSync = (p) => {
    90	      if (String(p) === '/repo/.handoff/lock') return { ...realLstat('/repo/.handoff'), isSymbolicLink: () => true, isDirectory: () => true, isFile: () => false };
    91	      return realLstat(p);
    92	    };
    93	    return io;
    94	  }
    95	
    96	  it('withLock refuses to acquire through a symlinked lock dir', () => {
    97	    const io = symlinkedLockIo();
    98	    let rmCalled = false;
    99	    const realRm = io.fs.rmSync.bind(io.fs);
   100	    io.fs.rmSync = (p, opts) => {
   101	      rmCalled = true;
   102	      return realRm(p, opts);
   103	    };
   104	    assert.throws(() => withLock('/repo', io, () => 'nope'), /symlink|jail|unsafe/i);
   105	    assert.equal(rmCalled, false, 'nothing is removed through the link');
   106	  });
   107	
   108	  it('recoverLock refuses a symlinked lock dir even with --force', () => {
   109	    const io = symlinkedLockIo();
   110	    const r = recoverLock('/repo', io, { force: true });
   111	    assert.equal(r.recovered, false);
   112	    assert.match(r.refusedReason, /symlink|jail|unsafe/i);
   113	  });
   114	});

    16	// The ten user-facing commands are the frozen v1 contract; session-start is an
    17	// adapter-facing addition prescribed by gate-2 finding 8 (the cheap
    18	// SessionStart gate Codex/Cursor hooks call — always exit 0).

    20	  'checkpoint',

    29	  'recover',
    30	  'session-start',
    31	];
    32	
    33	function version() {
    34	  const pkgPath = join(dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url)), '..', '..', 'package.json');
    35	  return JSON.parse(readFileSync(pkgPath, 'utf8')).version;
    36	}
    37	
    38	const USAGE = `usage: baton <command> [options]
    39	
    40	commands:
    41	  ${COMMANDS.join(', ')}
    42	
    43	global options:
    44	  --version   print version
    45	  --json      machine-readable output: one compact JSON envelope on stdout
    46	`;
    47	
    48	/**
    49	 * The --json contract: exactly one compact envelope line on stdout, nothing else.
    50	 * @param {any} io
    51	 * @param {{ok: boolean, data?: any, warnings?: any[], error?: {code: string, msg: string} | null}} env
    52	 */
    53	function emitEnvelope(io, env) {
    54	  const full = { ok: env.ok, data: env.data ?? null, warnings: env.warnings ?? [], error: env.error ?? null };
    55	  io.stdout.write(JSON.stringify(full) + '\n');
    56	}
    57	
    58	/**
    59	 * @param {string[]} argv process args after the binary
    60	 * @param {any} io injected {cwd, stdout, stderr}
    61	 * @returns {number | Promise<number>} exit code
    62	 */
    63	export function run(argv, io) {
    64	  const args = argv.filter((a) => a !== '');
    65	  // Global --json intent is parsed BEFORE dispatch (gate-2 iter-2 M6): a
    66	  // top-level `--json` with no command, or --help/--version, must still emit
    67	  // exactly one envelope rather than raw text.
    68	  const jsonWanted = args.includes('--json');
    69	  const cmdIndex = args.findIndex((a) => !a.startsWith('--'));
    70	  const cmd = cmdIndex === -1 ? undefined : args[cmdIndex];
    71	
    72	  if (cmd === undefined) {
    73	    // No command — just flags (or nothing).
    74	    if (args.includes('--version')) {
    75	      if (jsonWanted) emitEnvelope(io, { ok: true, data: { version: version() } });
    76	      else io.stdout.write(version() + '\n');
    77	      return 0;
    78	    }
    79	    if (args.includes('--help')) {
    80	      if (jsonWanted) emitEnvelope(io, { ok: true, data: { usage: USAGE.trim() } });
    81	      else io.stdout.write(USAGE);
    82	      return 0;
    83	    }
    84	    // Bare invocation (or a lone --json): a usage error.
    85	    if (jsonWanted) emitEnvelope(io, { ok: false, error: { code: 'usage', msg: 'no command given' } });
    86	    else io.stdout.write(USAGE);
    87	    return 2;
    88	  }
    89	
    90	  // All flags (before and after the command token) reach the command; only the
    91	  // command token itself is stripped, so a leading `--json` isn't lost.
    92	  const rest = args.filter((_, i) => i !== cmdIndex);
    93	
    94	  // Usage errors honor the --json envelope contract too (gate-2 fix 11 + M6:
    95	  // global --json counts, wherever it sits).
    96	  const wantsJson = jsonWanted;
    97	  if (cmd === 'wrap') {
    98	    if (wantsJson) emitEnvelope(io, { ok: false, error: { code: 'usage', msg: 'baton wrap is reserved for a future release (PTY supervisor); not available in v1' } });
    99	    else io.stderr.write(`baton wrap is reserved for a future release (PTY supervisor); not available in v1.\n${USAGE}`);
   100	    return 2;
   101	  }
   102	  if (!COMMANDS.includes(cmd)) {
   103	    if (wantsJson) emitEnvelope(io, { ok: false, error: { code: 'usage', msg: `unknown command '${cmd}'` } });
   104	    else io.stderr.write(`baton: unknown command '${cmd}'\n${USAGE}`);
   105	    return 2;
   106	  }
   107	
   108	  if (rest.includes('--help')) {
   109	    // Subcommand help honors the global --json envelope too (iter-3 F8): with
   110	    // --json it must emit exactly one envelope, never raw usage text.
   111	    const usage = `usage: baton ${cmd} [options]\n\nSee README for the ${cmd} contract.`;
   112	    if (wantsJson) emitEnvelope(io, { ok: true, data: { usage } });
   113	    else io.stdout.write(`${usage}\n`);
   114	    return 0;
   115	  }
   116	
   117	  if (cmd === 'status') return cmdStatus(rest, io);
   118	  if (cmd === 'detect') return cmdDetect(rest, io);
   119	  if (cmd === 'remap') return cmdRemap(rest, io);
   120	  if (cmd === 'checkpoint') return cmdCheckpoint(rest, io);
   121	  if (cmd === 'finalize') return cmdFinalize(rest, io);
   122	  if (cmd === 'receive') return cmdReceive(rest, io);
   123	  if (cmd === 'init') return cmdInit(rest, io);
   124	  if (cmd === 'doctor') return cmdDoctor(rest, io);
   125	  if (cmd === 'purge-transcript') return cmdPurgeTranscript(rest, io);
   126	  if (cmd === 'session-start') return cmdSessionStart(rest, io);
   127	  if (cmd === 'recover') return cmdRecover(rest, io);
   128	
   129	  const error = { code: 'not-implemented', msg: `baton ${cmd}: not implemented yet` };
   130	  if (rest.includes('--json')) {
   131	    emitEnvelope(io, { ok: false, error });
   132	  } else {
   133	    io.stderr.write(`${error.msg}\n`);
   134	  }
   135	  return 1;
   136	}
